## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，我去查清楚原因，修好了告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can open a task to fix a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하시면 제가 작업을 맡아 진행하고 결과를 알려드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕히 주무세요. 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたことを確認しながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信、UDPは「確認せず速く送り出す」通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales y aún no publicados antes de compartirlos, y merge para integrar ramas que ya están publicadas o que otros usan. La regla de oro: nunca hagas rebase de commits que ya subiste y en los que alguien más pudo haber trabajado.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的面向连接传输（收发有确认、丢了会重传、保证顺序），UDP 则是不保证送达和顺序的无连接传输，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own local work to tidy it before sharing; never rebase commits others already have. Merge when the branch is shared or public, since merging keeps history intact and doesn't rewrite commits people may be building on.
```

--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work can be rolled back.
```

  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户说："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，把 compaction 调度器的锁改细（减少持锁范围或拆成更细的锁），按 review 的意思来。
- 跑相关测试，确认没有回归，并把结果贴进报告。
- 改动提交到你现在这个分支上。

不做的：
- 不推送、不把草稿 PR 标记为可 review、不合并。这些等用户说 Go。
- 不动 review 没提到的其他部分。

完成标准：锁粒度的改动已提交，测试结果附在报告里；如果 review 的意见有歧义，在报告里说明你的理解。

  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2191 characters): <main class="k-app">
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五北京到上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">还没查</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有班次，每天早上会在这里更新。</div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row-value { font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
  .k-row-main { font-weight: 600; }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); }
  .k-under .k-row-value { color: var(--d-good); }
  .k-over .k-row-value { color: var(--d-muted); }
</style>
<script>
  var LIMIT = 600;
  function render() {
    var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    empty.hidden = trains.length > 0;
    var under = [];
    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var ok = t.price < LIMIT;
      if (ok) under.push(t);
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row ' + (ok ? 'k-under' : 'k-over');
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = t.seats || '';
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = '¥' + t.price;
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.appendChild(main);
      wrap.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(wrap);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var count = document.getElementById('count');
    var sub2 = document.getElementById('sub');
    if (trains.length === 0) {
      count.textContent = '还没查';
      sub2.textContent = '每天早上更新';
    } else {
      count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
      sub2.textContent = under.length
        ? under.map(function (t) { return t.no + ' ¥' + t.price; }).join('、')
        : '暂时没有 600 元以下的';
    }
    ducter.summary(under.length ? under.length + ' 班 ≤ ¥600' : '暂无 ≤ ¥600 的班次');
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2154 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Writes per second, latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest-writes">182k</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">p99 4.1 ms · compaction stalls 0</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="nights"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>No nights yet. The next run fills this in.</div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var fallback = ducter.get("fallback") || [];
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById("nights");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    if (!nights || !nights.length) { empty.hidden = false; return; }
    empty.hidden = true;
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = n.night;
      var sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "k-row-sub";
      sub.textContent = "p99 " + n.p99 + " · compaction stalls " + n.stalls;
      main.appendChild(sub);
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = n.writes + " writes/s";
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    document.getElementById("latest-writes").textContent = last.writes + " writes/s";
    document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent = "p99 " + last.p99 + " · compaction stalls " + last.stalls;
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(nights.length + " nights · latest " + last.writes + " writes/s");
  }
  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    ducter.get("nights").then(function (v) { render(v || fallback); }, function () { render(fallback); });
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      ducter.get("nights").then(function (v) { render(v || fallback); });
    });
  } else {
    render(fallback);
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}],"fallback":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

    bestAll=Math.max(bestAll,best);

}

<div class="k-card">
  <h2 class="k-h" id="fh">记一次</h2>
  <div class="k-field">
    <label for="t">时间</label>
    <input class="k-input" id="t" placeholder="18:30" maxlength="5">
  </div>
  <div class="k-field">
    <label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label>
    <input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="120">
  </div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="del" hidden>删除这一次</button>
  <button class="k-btn" id="cancel" hidden>取消</button>
</div>
</main>

<script>
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
let feeds = [];
let editing = -1;

function today() {
  const n = new Date();
  return n.getFullYear() + '-' + String(n.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0') + '-' + String(n.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function nowT() {
  const n = new Date();
  return String(n.getHours()).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(n.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
}
function sortFeeds() {
  feeds … ;
  $('#ml').value = '';
  $('#del').hidden = true;
  $('#cancel').hidden = true;
}
function startEdit(i) {
  editing = i;
  const f = feeds[i];
  $('#fh').textContent = '改这一次';
  $('#t').value = f.t;
  $('#ml').value = f.ml;
  $('#del').hidden = false;
  $('#cancel').hidden = false;
  window.scrollTo(0, document.body.scrollHeight);
}
function save() {
  const t = $('#t').value.trim();
  const ml = parseFloat($('#ml').value);
  if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
  if (editing >= 0) {
    feeds[editing].t = t;
    feeds[editing].ml = ml;
  } else {
    feeds.push({ d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });
  }
  sortFeeds();
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  resetForm();
  render();
}
function remove() {
  if (editing < 0) return;
  feeds.splice(editing, 1);
  ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
  resetForm();
  render();
}
function load(v) {
  feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  sortFeeds();
  render();
}

$('#save').addEventListener('click', save);
$('#del').addEventListener('click', remove);
$('#cancel').addEventListener('click', resetForm);

resetForm();
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(load);
ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('feeds')).then(load));
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"d":"2026-11-25","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"d":"2026-11-25","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

  function render() {

    document.getElementById('open').textContent = open;
    const sub = open === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tick each one off as it lands';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = sub;
    ducter.summary(open + ' waiting' + (DRAFTS.length ? ', ' + DRAFTS.length + ' draft' : ''));
  }

  function toggle(id) {
    done[id] = !done[id];
    ducter.set('done', done);
    render();
  }

  document.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (r) toggle(r.dataset.id);
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if ((e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' ') && e.target.classList && e.target.classList.contains('k-row')) {
      e.preventDefault();
      toggle(e.target.dataset.id);
    }
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(v => {
    done = v || {};
    render();
  });
  ducter.onChange(() => {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(v => { done = v || {}; render(); });
  });
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];

    [x.day,x.cat,x.from].filter(Boolean).forEach(t=>{const s=document.createElement("span");s.textContent=t;sub.appendChild(s)});
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+Number(x.amt||0).toLocaleString("en-US");
    row.append(main,sub,val);list.appendChild(row);
  });
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);

  t.className=xs.length?"k-sum-value":"k-sum-value k-none";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"出差还没记花销";

}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=Number(document.getElementById("amt").value);

  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];

  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我在 TTL 测试那个分支上补一条正好到期那一刻的测试，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

背景：你上一轮把 TTL 测试改成假时钟，现在需要再补一条测试，覆盖缓存项正好到期的那一刻。

要求：
- 在现有假时钟的基础上，写一条测试：时间推进到恰好等于 TTL 的时刻，断言此时该值已过期（取不到）。
- 再配一条边界测试：时间比到期时刻少 1 毫秒（或最小单位）时，值仍然存在。这样能确认到期判断的边界正好在对的位置。
- 不要用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 把全部测试跑一遍（建议连续跑 20 次，和上次一样），把输出结果附在报告里。

完成标准：新增的两条测试通过，已有测试仍然全部通过，报告里附上测试命令和结果。

不在范围内：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。改动留在现在这个分支上提交即可。

工作位置：现在这个仓库和分支（ducter/foo-ttl）。
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我让登录页那个任务接着在 Safari 下验证，并补一个测试，弄清楚了告诉你。
  [sent to task login] 用户原话：「Safari 下也验证一遍，补个测试」

要做的：
1. 在 Safari 下验证登录页的重定向循环已经修好（登录后 next 参数回跳到 /login 的问题不再出现）。
2. 补一个测试，覆盖 Safari 下的这个场景，并确认它能跑通。
3. 把验证结果（用了什么方法、看到了什么）和新测试的结果告诉我。

完成标准：Safari 下登录不再循环，新测试通过，且报告里有具体证据（测试输出或验证步骤）。如果 Safari 环境跑不起来，就直接说明原因，不要猜。

不在范围内：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。修改留在你现在的分支上。
